  "automatically_repaired_code": "apis.supervisor.complete_task(answer=total_likes)",
  "complete_environment_observation": "<REDACTED:CREDENTIAL_OBSERVATION:SHA256=893e75a4b2a311f8352fcdd7f2742d70db1e1b4e0fea4d0ba8bacd1e1a4eb152>",
  "exact_executed_code": "apis.supervisor.complete_task(answer=total_likes)",
  "exact_model_message_array": [
    {
      "content": "I am your supervisor and you are a super intelligent AI Assistant whose job is to achieve my day-to-day tasks completely autonomously.\n\nTo do this, you will need to interact with app/s (e.g., spotify, venmo etc) using their associated APIs on my behalf.   "prompt_tokens": 6544,
  "raw_model_response": "Now that I've adjusted the `page_limit` to a valid value, I'll retrieve all transactions Paul received this year using the `show_transactions` API with the updated parameters. I'll use pagination to ensure I get all transactions.\n\nCode:\n```python\ntransactions = []\npage_index = 0\npage_limit = 20  # Set a reasonable page limit within the allowed range\n\nwhile True:\n    transaction_page = apis.venmo.show_transactions(\n        access_token=<REDACTED:CREDENTIAL:SHA256=f542cec32c125ef1c0417e3dfcceb11c888f2a9f70c2c74265864412c0b7e859>'access_token'],\n        direction=\"received\",\n        min_created_at=min_created_at,\n        max_created_at=max_created_at,\n        page_index=page_index,\n        page_limit=page_limit\n    )\n    if not transaction_page:\n        break\n    transactions.extend(transaction_page)\n    page_index += 1\n\ntotal_likes = sum(transaction['like_count'] for transaction in transactions)\nprint(f\"Total likes on all received transactions this year: {total_likes}\")\n```",

      "content": "Now that I've adjusted the `page_limit` to a valid value, I'll retrieve all transactions Paul received this year using the `show_transactions` API with the updated parameters. I'll use pagination to ensure I get all transactions.\n\nCode:\n```python\ntransactions = []\npage_index = 0\npage_limit = 20  # Set a reasonable page limit within the allowed range\n\nwhile True:\n    transaction_page = apis.venmo.show_transactions(\n        access_token=<REDACTED:CREDENTIAL:SHA256=f542cec32c125ef1c0417e3dfcceb11c888f2a9f70c2c74265864412c0b7e859>'access_token'],\n        direction=\"received\",\n        min_created_at=min_created_at,\n        max_created_at=max_created_at,\n        page_index=page_index,\n        page_limit=page_limit\n    )\n    if not transaction_page:\n        break\n    transactions.extend(transaction_page)\n    page_index += 1\n\ntotal_likes = sum(transaction['like_count'] for transaction in transactions)\nprint(f\"Total likes on all received transactions this year: {total_likes}\")\n```",
      "role": "assistant"
